Se. { -jɛ̃st- } in / haˈtejɛ̃sthaɁ / 'he's trying to learn it, he's reading'
(cf. Chafe 1967:87).
Hu. { -jẽhst- }, cf. <gaïensti> 'Enseigner' (MS 59:81), / h / assumed present.
⁴⁸³

PNI * { -wjẽhw- }, * { -wjẽw- }, * { -wjẽɁ- } 'know how, learn how'

Tu. { -wjə̃w- } in / ˈraːwjə̃ws / 'he learns' (cf. Rudes 1999:488).

Mo. { -wejʌ̃w- } in / keweˈjʌ̃ːhũ / 'I am good at, know how to, have a knack for'
(cf. Maracle 1990:44).
Oo. { -WjẽɁ- } in / hóːˈjẽɁih / 'he has learned how, he is accomplished'
(cf. Woodbury 2003:932).
Se. { -jɛ̃Ɂ- } in / akejɛ̃ɁˈheɁɔ̃h / 'I've learned it' (cf. Chafe 1967:87).
Hu. { -jẽhw- }, cf. <gaïenh8i> 'Savoir faire q.c.' (MS 59:80).
Wy. { -ʒɛ̃ːw- } in / jeˈʒɛ̃ːwih / 'I know how' (cf. Barbeau 1960:262).
⁴⁸⁴

PNI * { -wjẽn- } 'craft, manner, skill, way'
Tu. { -wjə̃n- } in / oˈwjə̃ːnɛh / 'craft, manner, skill' (cf. Rudes 1999:488),
perhaps a borrowed word.
Mo. { -wejʌ̃n- } in / oweˈjʌ̃ːnaɁ / 'conduct, mien, posture, way of doing, acting'
(cf. Maracle 1990:76).

556

Oe. { -wjʌ̃n- } in / kewjʌ̃nʌ̃ːˈtâːseɁ / 'I'm getting something ready'
(cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:777).
Oo. { -Wjẽn- } in / káːˈjẽːnaɁ / 'task, manner, way' (cf. Woodbury 2003:924).
Ca. { -wajẽn- } in / aɁewajẽːˈnẽːtaɁ / 'she finished' (cf. Froman et al. 2002:581).
Hu. { -jẽn- } in / xaˈjẽːnaɁ / <gaïenda> 'maniere dagir b.' (MS 59:80).
⁴⁸⁵

PMO * { -wjʌ̃tehtaɁ- } 'become capable, learn how'
Mo. { -wejʌ̃tehtaɁ- } in / kewejʌ̃ˈtehtaɁs / 'I become capable, I make progress'
(cf. Maracle 1990:282).
Oe. { -wjʌ̃tehtaɁ- } in / ʌ̃hsewjʌ̃ˈtehtaneɁ / 'you will learn'
(cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:829).

⁴⁸⁶

PI * { -wohst } 'be good'
Ch. { -ȍːhst- } in / őːhstâː / 'good, well' (cf. Feeling & Pulte 1975:150).

PNI * { -wahstih }, * { -akwahst } 'be good'; syllable * / ak / anomalous or
perhaps original.
Tu. { -akwahst } in / ˈwakwahst / 'it is good' (cf. Rudes 1999:590).
No. * { -akwahst } in <Waquast> 'Good' (cf. Rudes 1981a:47).
Se. { -akwast- } in / oˈkwastɔ̃h / 'it's auspicious' (cf. Chafe 1967:68).

Hu. { -wahstih }, cf. <ga8asti> 'avoir beau Temps' (MS 59:124),
/ h / assumed present.
Wy. { -wahstih } in / jaˈwahstih / 'it is good' (cf. Barbeau 1960:78).
⁴⁸⁷

PNI * { -wɹ- } 'air, wind'
Tu. { -wr- } in / ˈowrɛh / 'air, wind' (cf. Rudes 1999:482).
Mo. { -weɹ- } in / oˈweːɹaɁ / 'air, wind, thunder' (cf. Maracle 1990:76).
Oe. { -wel- } in / ˈowelaɁ / 'air, wind' (cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:768).
Oo. { -R- } in / ˈóːwæɁ / 'wind' (cf. Woodbury 2003:69).
Ca. {-R- } in / oˈwaːnoː / 'it is a cold wind' (cf. Froman et al. 2002:60).
Se. { -R- } in / tejoæwɛ̃ːnjɔ̃ːh / 'the breeze is stirring' (cf. Chafe 1967:47).
Hu. { -ɹ- } in / ˈoɹaɁ / <ora> 'vent, air' (MS 59:131).

⁴⁸⁸

PI * { -Ɂn- } 'arrow'
Ch. { -Ɂn- } in / káɁni ̋ː / 'bullet, lead' (cf. Feeling & Pulte 1975:108);
according to King (1975:191), the Cherokee term also refers to Mountain
Cane (Phyllostachys violescens), from which arrow shafts were made.

557

PNI * { -Ɂn- } 'arrow'.
Tu. { -Ɂt- } in / ˈaɁtɛh / 'arrow, bullet, dart, projectile' (cf. Rudes 1999:112).
Ca. { -Ɂn- } in / kɁanõh / 'arrow' (cf. Froman et al. 2002:15),
borrowed from Seneca.
Se. { -Ɂn- } in / kaɁnɔ̃Ɂ / 'arrow' (cf. Chafe 1967:91).

Hu. { -Ɂn- } in / ˈxaɁnaɁ / <ganda> 'fléche' (MS 59:84), / Ɂ / assumed present.
Wy. { -Ɂn- } in / ˈuɁnaɁ / 'arrow' (cf. Barbeau 1960:154).
[ cf. Mithun 1979:181, Mithun 1984b:269 ]
⁴⁸⁹

PNI * { -Ɂnẽj- } 'bone'
Oo. { -Ɂnẽj- } in / óɁˈnẽːjaɁ / 'human remains' (cf. Woodbury 2003:948).
Se. { -Ɂnɛ̃j- } in / oɁnɛ̃ːjaɁ / 'bone' (cf. Chafe 1967:91).
Hu. { -ɁnẽJ- } in / oɁˈnẽːaɁ / <onnea> 'os' (MS 59:109),
/ Ɂ / and / ẽ / assumed present.

Wy. { -Ɂnɛ̃J- } in / uɁˈnɛ̃ːaɁ / 'bone' (cf. Barbeau 1960:157).
⁴⁹⁰

PNI * { -Ɂnhõhs- } 'egg'
Tu. { -Ɂnhə̃hs- } in / oɁˈnhə̃hsɛh / 'egg, testicle' (cf. Rudes 1999:528).
Mo. { -Ɂnhũhs- } in / oɁˈnhũhsaɁ / 'egg' (cf. Maracle 1990:58).
Oe. { -Ɂnhũhs- } in / oɁˈnhũhsaɁ / 'egg' (cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:853).
Oo. { -Ɂnhũhs- } in / óɁˈnhũhsaɁ / 'egg' (cf. Woodbury 2003:948).
Ca. { -Ɂnhõhs- } in / oɁˈnhõhsaɁ / 'eggs' (cf. Froman et al. 2002:102).

Se. { -Ɂhɔ̃hs- } in / oɁˈhɔ̃hsaɁ / 'egg' (cf. Chafe 1967:90).

Hu. { -Ɂnhõhʃ- } in / oɁˈnhõhʃaɁ / <Onnhonchia> 'ovum' (MS 59:112),
/ Ɂ / and preconsonantal / h / assumed present.
Wy. { -tɔ̃hʃ- } in / uˈtɔ̃hʃaɁ / 'egg' (cf. Barbeau 1960:274).
⁴⁹¹

PNI * { -Ɂnikõhɹ- } 'mind, spirit'
Tu. { -Ɂtikə̃hr- } in / oɁtiˈkə̃hrɛh / 'mind, reason, temper' (cf. Rudes 1999:184).
Mo. { -Ɂnikũhɹ- } in / oɁniˈkũ̂ːɹaɁ / 'spirit, character, thought, opinion, brain,
mind, belief, intention' (cf. Maracle 1990:598).
Oe. { -Ɂnikũhl- } in / oɁniˈkũ̂ːlaɁ / 'mind, spirit'
(cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:855).
Oo. { -ɁnikũhR- } in / kaɁníˈkũhæːɁ / 'mind, thought, spirit'
(cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:952).
Ca. { -ɁnikõhR- } in / kɁaˈnikõhaɁ / 'the mind' (cf. Froman et al. 2002:200).

558

Se. { -Ɂnikɔ̃HR- } in / hoɁˈnikɔ̃ɛɁ̃ / 'his mind' (cf. Chafe 1967:91).
Hu. { -Ɂnixõhɹ- } in / xaɁniˈxõhɹaɁ / <gandigon‛ra> 'esprit, ame, pensée'
(MS 59:100), / Ɂ / assumed present.
Wy. { -Ɂnijɔ̃ːɹ- } in / huɁnijɔ̃ːɹuˈwaːnɛ̃h / 'his mind is great'
(cf. Barbeau 1960:266).
⁴⁹²

PNI * { -Ɂnitsko- }, * { -Ɂnitskʷ- } 'be late, delay'
Tu. { -Ɂtiθko- } in / joɁˈtiθkoɁ / 'it is late' (cf. Rudes 1999:536),
{ -Ɂtiθkʷ- } in / roɁˈtiθkʷahs / 'he delays' (cf. Rudes 1999:573).
Mo. { -Ɂnihskʷ- } in / wakeɁˈnihskʷahs / 'I am late (not on time)'
(cf. Maracle 1990:363).
Oe. { -Ɂniskʷ- } in / joɁˈniskʷʌ̃Ɂ / 'it's late' (cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:861).
Oo. { -Ɂniskʷ- } in / óɁˈniskʷẽh / 'it is late' (cf. Woodbury 2003:964).
Ca. { -Ɂnihsko- } in / ẽˈwakhnihskoɁ / 'I will be late'
(cf. Froman et al. 2002:178).

⁴⁹³

PNI * { -ɁnjakẽɁ- } 'escape, flee, run away'
Mo. { -Ɂnjakʌ̃Ɂ- } in / keɁˈnjaːkʌ̃Ɂs / 'I run away, escape' (cf. Maracle 1990:38).
Oe. { -Ɂnjakʌ̃Ɂ- } in / loɁnjaˈkʌ̃ː̂ ũ / 'he has run away'
(cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:864).
Oo. { -ɁnjakẽɁ- } in / kéɁˈnjaːkẽɁs / 'I escape, I run away'
(cf. Woodbury 2003:969).

Ca. { -ɁnjakẽɁ- } in / keɁˈnjaːkẽɁs / 'I'm an escaper' (cf. Froman et al. 2002:107).
Se. { -Ɂnjakɛ̃Ɂ- } in / akeɁnjaˈkɛ̃Ɂɔ̃h / 'I've escaped, run away' (cf. Chafe 1967:92).
Hu. { -ɁnjaxẽɁ- }, cf. <gandiagendi> 'Séchapper des Ennemis' (MS 59:96),
/ Ɂ / assumed present.
Wy. { -Ɂnjajɛ̃Ɂ- } in / nusahaˈtiɁnjajɛ̃haɁ / 'that they escaped again'
(cf. Barbeau 1960:311).
⁴⁹⁴

PI * { -Ɂnjõːhs- } 'nose'
Ch. { -Ɂjʌ̃ː̀ hs- } in / khàjʌ̃ː̀ hsőːlîː / 'his nose' (cf. Feeling & Pulte 1975:143).
PNI * { -Ɂnjõhs- } 'nose'.

Tu. { -Ɂtjə̃hs- } in / ˈktjə̃hsɛh / 'my nose' (cf. Rudes 1999:537).
No. * { -Ɂtjõhs- } in <Oteusag> 'The nose' (cf. Rudes 1981a:46).
Mo. { -Ɂnjũhs- } in / oɁˈnjũhsaɁ / 'nose' (cf. Maracle 1990:58).
Oe. { -Ɂnjũhs- } in / oɁˈnjũhsaɁ / 'nose' (cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:866).

559

Oo. { -Ɂnjũhs- } in / keɁnjṹhˈsaɁkeh / 'my nose' (cf. Woodbury 2003:970).
Ca. { -Ɂnjõhs- } in / oɁˈnjõhsaɁ / 'nose' (cf. Froman et al. 2002:213).
Se. { -Ɂnjɔ̃hs- } in / oɁˈnjɔ̃hsaɁ / 'nose' (cf. Chafe 1967:92).
[ cf. Rudes 1995:50 ]
⁴⁹⁵

PNI * { -Ɂnonsh- } 'be envious, be jealous, envy'
Tu. { -Ɂtoθh- } in / raɁˈtoːθhahs / 'he begrudges, he envies' (cf. Rudes 1999:536).
Mo. { -Ɂnosh- } in / keɁˈnoshahs / 'I envy, am envious of, I am jealous'
(cf. Maracle 1990:42).
Oo. { -Ɂnũsh- } in / hũwáɁˈnũshas / 'he or she envies him'
(cf. Woodbury 2003:967), change of vowel anomalous.
Ca. { -Ɂnosh- } in / seɁˈnoːshahs / 'you are envious' (cf. Froman et al. 2002:170).
Se. { -Ɂnosh- } in / koɁnoːshɛ̃ːh / 'she's jealous' (cf. Chafe 1967:91).
Hu. { -Ɂnosh- }, cf. <gand8sandi> 'porter envie a qlq.' (MS 59:105),
dative-benefactive form, / Ɂ / and / h / assumed present.

⁴⁹⁶

PNI * { -Ɂnshanst- } 'be powerful, be strong'
Tu. { -Ɂθhaθn- } in / kaɁˈθhaːθnɛɁ / 'it is potent, it is powerful'
(cf. Rudes 1999:273).
Mo. { -Ɂshatst- } in / joɁˈshatsteɁ / 'it is strong, tough, durable, brutal, violent'
(cf. Maracle 1990:17).
Oe. { -Ɂshatst- } in / keɁˈshatsteɁ / 'I'm strong'
(cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:867).

Oo. { -Ɂshast- } in / hoɁshastẽhseːˈwaːnẽh / 'the Creator' [lit. 'his power is great']
(cf. Woodbury 2003:548).
Ca. { -Ɂshahst- } in / tshẽh niˈkaɁshahsteɁ / 'exponents'
(cf. Froman et al. 2002:387).
Hu. { -Ɂshast- }, cf. <gasaste> 'une ch. etre chere, qlq. vend. cher' (MS 59:147),
/ Ɂ / and / h / assumed present.
⁴⁹⁷

PNI * { -ɁtskoɁ- } 'drown, fall into water'

Tu. { -ɁθkoɁ- } in / ˈraɁθkoɁθ / 'he drowns' (cf. Rudes 1999:274).
Mo. { -ɁskoɁ- } in / ˈkêːskoɁs / 'I drown, fall into the water'
(cf. Maracle 1990:38).
Oe. { -ɁskoɁ- } in / waˈkêːskoneɁ / 'I fell in the water'
(cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:869).

560

Oo. { -ɁskoɁ- } in / hóɁˈskoɁih / 'he has drowned, he has fallen into the water'
(cf. Woodbury 2003:975).
Ca. { -ɁskoɁ- } in / ahõwaˈtiɁskoɁ / 'they (m) have baptized him, them'
(cf. Froman et al. 2002:387).
Se. { -ɁskoɁ- } in / oɁˈkeɁskoɁt / 'I fell in the water' (cf. Chafe 1967:93).

Hu. { -ɁskoɁ- }, cf. <gasko> 'tomb. a leau, enfoucer, fe. naufrage' (MS 59:149),
/ Ɂ / assumed present.
Wy. { -ɁskuɁ- } in / eˈheɁskuhk / 'I will drown him' (cf. Barbeau 1960:249).
⁴⁹⁸

PNI * { -Ɂtsɹ- } 'drag, drive, pull, ride'
Tu. { -Ɂθr- } in / ˈroɁθreːɁ / 'he rode' (cf. Rudes 1999:272).
Mo. { -Ɂseɹ- } in / ˈkâːseɹeɁ / 'a vehicle, car, wagon, cart' (cf. Maracle 1990:135).
Oe. { -Ɂsl- } in / ˈlâːsleɁ / 'he's dragging it' (cf. Michelson & Doxtator 2002:870).
Oo. { -ɁsR- } in / káˈseːhtaɁ / 'car, vehicle' (cf. Woodbury 2003:971),
with causative.

Ca. { -Ɂtsɹ- } in / ɁaˈkeɁtsɹeːɁ / 'I dragged it', Lower Ca. / ɁaˈkeɁtɹeːɁ /
(cf. Froman et al. 2002:95).
Se. { -ɁsR- } in / aˈkeɁsjɔ̃ːh / 'I've dragged it' (cf. Chafe 1967:93).
